## The Union of Independent USFs on Z^d Is Transient

- 原件 f038f787ca463ebf1f55679791e3fa282384ddbf81f2d7134524a498b73d76ae。arXiv:2311.09183v1 (15 Nov 2023).。物理页 1, 2。
- independent USF unions。Abstract, introduction, and Theorem 1.1, PDF pp. 1–2.。
- 已有技能。For d≥3, the preprint’s Theorem 1.1 states that the union of an everywhere-percolating subgraph of Z^d with an independent epsilon-thinned WUSF is almost surely connected and transient; it records the union-of-independent-USFs consequence.。
- 适用范围。This is a Z^d, d≥3, independence and everywhere-percolating-set result. It does not resolve corresponding arbitrary-Cayley-graph questions or remove the thinning/independence hypotheses.。
- 相关技能。[probability-forests-electrical-networks](../../../../probability-forests-electrical-networks/SKILL.md)。

## Uniform Spanning Forests of Planar Graphs

- 原件 b0b28509f05baaa418e00cd0cf8cb58ca16dc5e22ef738a37bcac61bf1fe3f15。Tom Hutchcroft and Asaf Nachmias manuscript dated 24 Jan 2018; publication version not confirmed from inspected pages.。物理页 1, 2。
- planar USFs。Abstract and introduction, PDF pp. 1–2.。
- 已有技能。The manuscript states that the FUSF of every bounded-degree proper plane graph is almost surely connected and describes quantitative exponent results for transient proper plane graphs, formulated using circle-packing hyperbolic geometry.。
- 适用范围。The proper plane embedding, bounded degree/codegree, transience, and hyperbolic-circle-packing metric are essential; this does not yield connectivity for all planar graphs.。
- 相关技能。[probability-forests-electrical-networks](../../../../probability-forests-electrical-networks/SKILL.md)。

## Universality of High-Dimensional Spanning Forests and Sandpiles

- 原件 d5a4c15a73698fdf69379688f8a58707b17b852c83225d38b4029f54758afdcf。arXiv:1804.04120v2 (30 May 2019); manuscript dated 31 May 2019.。物理页 1, 2。
- high-dimensional WUSF and sandpiles。Abstract and contents, PDF pp. 1–2.。
- 已有技能。For a broad class including transitive graphs of at least quintic volume growth and bounded-degree nonamenable graphs, the paper states mean-field WUSF behavior, including spectral/walk dimensions and intrinsic/extrinsic past exponents, with Abelian-sandpile avalanche consequences.。
- 适用范围。The stated high-dimensional growth or nonamenability hypotheses, the WUSF object, and the definition of a vertex’s past must be preserved; no universal result for lower-dimensional or arbitrary graphs follows.。
- 相关技能。[probability-forests-electrical-networks](../../../../probability-forests-electrical-networks/SKILL.md)。
